Um... well, I really try not to let myself think that way, because I know I should be grateful for what I have. But... yes, I guess I have been feeling a lot of guilt lately. I'm sorry to even say it, I know I shouldn't be so hard on myself.

It's just... I keep replaying every little thing I feel like I've failed at. Like with my friend, or the choir, or even my own family. I just keep thinking about all the things I didn't do right, or the times I wasn't there for them enough. And... um... sometimes I just feel like I'm a completely useless person. Like I deserve whatever punishment is coming my way because I've let everyone down. 

I know that sounds terrible, and I really try to push those thoughts away, I promise. I don't want you to think I'm just sitting around throwing a pity party. Is it normal to feel that guilty over little things? I hope I'm not overreacting.

Like the thoughts just stop. \n\nI know it sounds silly, and I'm probably making a bigger deal out of it than it is. I just stand there for a minute and try to catch up. Does that help? I want to make sure I'm answering your question the way you need me to.\n\nDoctor: When this happens, do you later have a gap in your memory for what happened during that moment?\n\nPatient: Um, no, I don't think so. Um... well, I really try my best to keep up with everything, I promise I do. I don't want to be a bother or make anyone wait on me.

Um... well, I really hope I'm not making it sound worse than it is. I've been trying so hard to just push through it and do my job and be there for my family. I don't want you to think I'm just giving up or anything.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um... well, I really hope I'm not making a big deal out of nothing. I mean, everyone forgets things sometimes, right? I don't want you to think I'm losing my mind or that I can't do my job. I really try to stay on top of everything.

But... I guess that's actually kind of why I came in. It's just... my mind keeps going blank at the absolute worst times. Like, I'll be looking at the road-closure forms, or holding my stop-slow paddle, and suddenly... I just can't remember the steps. And I've been doing this for years! It's so embarrassing. 

I really try to cover it up so no one notices, and I always figure it out eventually, I promise. I'm not putting anyone in danger. It's just... it's been scaring me a little bit, to be honest. Is that... is it normal to have moments like that when you're under a lot of stress? I really hope I'm not failing at my job.
